its fair value could be provided by the management of         由本公司管理層提供或可根據活躍

price in an active market). For loan receivables that the     理 層 認 為 無 法 收 回 的 應 收 貸 款，不

     Risk management policies                                        風險管理政策
     The Group has developed a credit policy and procedures           本集團已就其放債業務制定信貸政
     manual for its money lending business. The credit policy         策 及 程 序 手 冊。信 貸 政 策 及 程 序 手
     and procedures manual specifies, among others, the               冊 訂 明（ 其 中 包 括）貸 款 申 請、信 貸
     loan application, credit approval and monitoring ongoing         審 批 及 監 控 持 續 信 貸 風 險 程 序。向
     credit risk procedures. Prior to grant of a loan to a            借 款 人 授 出 貸 款 前，本 集 團 會 對 借
     borrower, the Group carries out credit risk assessment           款 人 進 行 信 貸 風 險 評 估，當 中 計 及
     on the borrower, taking into account, among other               （ 其 中 包 括）借 款 人 之 背 景 及 品 格、
     things, the background and character of the borrowers,           股 東 背 景、品 格 及 管 理 層 能 力（ 如
     shareholders’ background, character, and management              有）、貸 款 目 的、抵 押 品 及 擔 保 價 值
     capability (if any), purpose of the loan, value of collateral   （ 如 有）、（ 倘 適 用）相 關 公 開 檢 索 以
     and guarantee (if any), where applicable, relevant public        及 借 款 人、股 東 及 擔 保 人 之 財 務 實
     searches and the financial strength of the borrower,             力。
     shareholder and guarantor.

     contingent upon obtaining approval. The company               其 後 會 進 行 徹 底 審 閱，以 確 保 符 合
     secretary then undertakes a thorough review to ensure         所 有 相 關 上 市 規 則。倘 獲 確 認 屬 須
     compliance with all relevant Listing Rules. If confirmed      予 公 佈 交 易，該 建 議 連 同 所 有 相 關
     as a notifiable transaction, the proposal, along with         文件將正式提交董事會作最終批准。
     all associated documentation, is formally escalated to        此項強化了集中監督及規定預先批
     the Board for final approval. This enhanced policy and        准檢查的政策及程序，已全面實施，
     procedure, which centralizes oversight and mandates pre-      以減少任何潛在的不合規情況。
     approval checks, has been fully implemented to mitigate
     any potential instances of non-compliance.

     Practices and Conduct of Board Meetings                      董事會會議常規及召開方式
     The Board conducts meeting on a regular basis and on         董事會定期及以特定基準舉行會議。
     an adhoc basis. The Bye-laws allows Board meetings           公司細則准許董事會會議以電話或
     to be conducted by means of telephones or other              利 用 其 他 通 訊 設 備 進 行，以 及 於 必
     communications equipment and any resolutions to be           要時不時以經全體董事傳閱及簽署
     passed by way of written resolutions circulated to and       之 書 面 決 議 案 通 過 任 何 決 議 案，惟
     signed by all Directors from time to time when necessary     倘董事因健康欠佳或行動不便而暫
     unless such as are temporarily unable to act through ill-    時未能行事則作別論。
     health or disability.

     At least 14 days’ notice for regular Board meetings and      全體董事均就常規董事會會議及非
     reasonable notice for non-regular Board meetings are         常規董事會會議分別獲給予至少
     given to all Directors so as to ensure that each of them     十 四 日 之 通 知 及 合 理 的 通 知，以 確
     had an opportunity to attend the meetings. The company       保 各 董 事 皆 有 機 會 出 席 會 議。本 公
     secretary of the Company (the “Company Secretary”)           司 公 司 秘 書（「公 司 秘 書」）協 助 董 事
     assists the chairman of the Board in preparing the           會 主 席 編 製 會 議 議 程，並 確 保 所 有
     agenda for meetings and ensures that all applicable          適 用 規 則 及 規 例 獲 得 遵 守。議 程 及
     rules and regulations are complied with. The agenda          隨附之董事會文件在董事會會議之
     and the accompanying Board papers are sent in full to        日期前至少三天送呈全體董事。
     all Directors at least 3 days before the date of the Board
     meeting.

     After the meeting, draft minutes of the Board meeting        會 後，董 事 會 會 議 記 錄 之 初 稿 於 簽
     are circulated to all Directors for their comment before     立 批 准 前，先 供 所 有 董 事 傳 閱 並 提
     execution and approval. All Board minutes are kept by        出 意 見。所 有 董 事 會 會 議 記 錄 均 由
     the Company Secretary and available for inspection at        公 司 秘 書 保 存，倘 有 任 何 董 事 發 出
     any reasonable time on reasonable notice given by the        合 理 通 知，則 會 供 其 於 任 何 合 理 時
     Directors. Every Director is entitled to have access to      段 內 查 閱。每 名 董 事 均 有 權 查 閱 董
     Board papers and related materials and has access to the     事 會 文 件 及 相 關 資 料，並 可 向 公 司
     advice and services of the Company Secretary.                秘書尋求意見並獲其提供服務。

     Directors.

     basis including a review of the audit scope and the          圍 及 核 數 費 用；確 保 核 數 師 保 持 客

     WHISTLEBLOWING POLICY AND ANTI-                              舉報政策及反貪腐敗政策
     CORRUPTION POLICY
     The Company has established the whistleblowing policy        本 公 司 已 制 定 舉 報 政 策，容 許 所 有
     which allows all employees and independent third             員工及獨立第三方（ 包括客戶、供應
     parties, including customers, suppliers and contractors,     商及承包商）以匿名方式向董事會或
     to report any possible improprieties, misconducts,           審 核 委 員 會 舉 報 財 務 彙 報、內 部 監
     malpractices or irregularities in matters of financial       控或其他事宜中任何可能的不當行
     reporting, internal control or other matters to the Board    為、失 職 行 為、瀆 職 及 違 規 行 為。本
     or the Audit Committee anonymously. The Group will           集 團 將 謹 慎 處 理 舉 報 及 投 訴，並 公
     handle the reports and complaints with care and will         平 妥 當 地 對 待 舉 報 人 的 問 題。審 核
     treat the whistle-blower’s concerns fairly and properly.     委 員 會 對 舉 報 政 策 負 有 總 體 責 任，
     The Audit Committee has the overall responsibility for       但將監督及執行該政策的日常責任
     the whistleblowing policy but has delegated day-to-          委 託 予 指 定 職 員。任 何 被 發 現 傷 害
     day responsibility for overseeing and implementing it to     或報復根據本政策提出問題的人士
     a designated officer. Any person who is found to have        的，均將受到紀律處分。
     victimized or retaliated against those who have raised
     concerns under this policy will subject to disciplinary
     sanctions.

     The Group has adopted anti-corruption policy on a zero-      本集團已採納反腐敗政策，對任何形
     tolerance basis for any form of corruption, including        式的貪污（ 包括賄賂及勒索、欺詐及
     bribery and extortion, fraud and money laundering, and       洗 黑 錢）採 取 零 容 忍 態 度，並 承 諾 以
     promise to operate our business in an honest, ethical        誠 實、合 乎 道 德 及 信 譽 的 方 式 經 營
     and creditable manner. The policies are revised in due       業務。本集團會適時修訂有關政策，
     course and all Directors and employees are reminded          並會不時提醒所有董事及僱員有關
     with its requirement from time to time. In particular, the   其 規 定。特 別 是，本 集 團 已 制 定 行 為
     Group has established a code of conduct and stipulated       準 則，並 於 本 公 司 的 合 規 手 冊 中 作
     in the compliance manual of the Company. Additionally,       出 規 定。此 外，本 集 團 已 就 內 幕 消 息
     the Group has implemented an effective internal manual       或價格敏感資料實施有效的內部手
     on inside information or price sensitive information and     冊，並為舉報任何可疑的業務違規、
     has provided separate channels for the reporting of any      欺詐和貪污行為提供獨立渠道。

OTHER INFORMATION                                             其他信息
The directors are responsible for the other information.       董 事 須 對 其 他 信 息 負 責。其 他 信 息
The other information comprises all of the information         包 括 年 報 內 的 所 有 資 料，但 不 包 括
included in the annual report, other than the consolidated     綜合財務報表及我們的核數師報告
financial statements and our auditor’s report thereon         （「其他信息」）。
(“other information”).

Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements          我們對綜合財務報表的意見並不涵
does not cover the other information and we do not            蓋 其 他 信 息，我 們 永 不 對 該 等 其 他
express any form of assurance conclusion thereon.             信息發表任何形式的鑒證結論。

In connection with our audit of the consolidated              結 合 我 們 對 綜 合 財 務 報 表 的 審 計，
financial statements, our responsibility is to read the       我 們 的 責 任 是 閱 讀 其 他 信 息，考 慮
other information and, in doing so, consider whether          其他信息是否與綜合財務報表或我
the other information is materially inconsistent with         們在審計過程中所了解的情況存在
the consolidated financial statements or our knowledge        重大抵觸或者似乎存在重大錯誤陳
obtained in the audit or otherwise appears to be              述 的 情 況。根 據 我 們 所 做 的 工 作，如
materially misstated. If, based on the work we have           果我們認為該其他資料存在重大錯
performed, we conclude that there is a material               誤 陳 述，則 我 們 須 報 告 該 事 實。我 們
misstatement of this other information, we are required       於此方面並無報告。
to report that fact. We have nothing to report in this
regard.

     1. GENERAL                                                                   1. 一般資料
         SunCorp Technologies Limited (the “Company”) was                          新 確 科 技 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）
         incorporated in Bermuda as an exempted company                            為根據百慕達公司法在百慕達

        INFORMATION
         These consolidated financial statements have been             該等綜合財務報表乃按歷史成

         below.

         The preparation of financial statements in conformity         編製符合香港財務報告準則的
         with HKFRS requires the use of certain critical               財務報表需要採用若干關鍵會
         accounting estimates. It also requires management             計估計。管理層亦須在採用本集
         to exercise its judgement in the process of applying          團會計政策的過程中作出判斷。
         the Group’s accounting policies. The areas involving          涉及高度判斷或極為複雜的範
         a higher degree of judgement or complexity, or areas          疇，或對綜合財務報表而言屬重
         where assumptions and estimates are significant to            大假設及估計的範疇，乃於附註

         note 5.

         unless otherwise stated.

         (a) Consolidation                                             (a) 綜合賬目
              The consolidated financial statements include              綜合財務報表包括本公司
              the financial statements of the Company and                及其附屬公司編製的截至
              its subsidiaries made up to 31 December.                   十二月三十一日止的財務
              Subsidiaries are entities over which the Group             報 表。附 屬 公 司 指 本 集 團 具
              has control. The Group controls an entity when             有 控 制 權 的 實 體。當 本 集 團
              it is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns          通過參與實體業務而享有
              from its involvement with the entity and has the           或 有 權 享 有 浮 動 回 報，並 有
              ability to affect those returns through its power          能力通過對實體的權力而
              over the entity. The Group has power over an               影 響 該 等 回 報 時，即 對 該 實
              entity when the Group has existing rights that             體 具 有 控 制 權。當 本 集 團 擁
              give it the current ability to direct the relevant         有令其目前能夠指揮相關
              activities, i.e. activities that significantly affect      活動（ 即對實體回報造成重
              the entity’s returns.                                      大 影 響 的 活 動）的 現 有 權 利
                                                                         時，本 集 團 即 對 該 實 體 擁 有

     probable that taxable profits will be available                             稅項虧損或未動用稅項抵

     constituting an event of default for internal                   表明符合以下任何一項標
     credit risk management purposes as historical                   準的應收賬款一般無法收
     experience indicates that receivables that meet                 回，故 就 內 部 信 貸 風 險 管 理
     either of the following criteria are generally not              而 言，以 下 情 況 構 成 違 約 事
     recoverable:                                                    件：

     –   when there is a breach of financial covenants               － 交易對手違反財務契
         by the counterparty; or                                       諾；或

     –   information developed internally or obtained                － 內部生成或從外部來
         from external sources indicates that the                      源 所 得 之 資 料 顯 示，債
         debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors,                      務人不大可能向債權
         including the Group, in full (without taking                  人（ 包 括 本 集 團 ）支 付
         into account any collaterals held by the                      全 數 款 項（ 在 不 計 及 本
         Group).                                                       集團所持之任何抵押品
                                                                       下）。

     Irrespective of the above analysis, the Group                   無 論 上 述 分 析 結 果 如 何，倘
     considers that default has occurred when a                      金 融 資 產 逾 期 超 過90日，本
     financial asset is more than 90 days past due                   集 團 將 視 作 已 發 生 違 約，除
     unless the Group has reasonable and supportable                 非本集團擁有合理且可靠
     information to demonstrate that a more lagging                  之資料證明較寬鬆之違約
     default criterion is more appropriate.                          標準更為適用，則當別論。

            Credit-impaired financial assets                              已信貸減值之金融資產

             A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or             當一項或多項對金融資產
             more events that have a detrimental impact on                之估計未來現金流量造成
             the estimated future cash flows of that financial            負 面 影 響 之 事 件 發 生 時，即
             asset have occurred. Evidence that a financial               代表金融資產已信貸減值。
             asset is credit-impaired includes observable data            信貸減值之證據包括涉及
             about the following events:                                  以下事件之可觀察數據：

             –   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or        － 發行人或交易對手陷入
                 the counterparty;                                          嚴重財困；

             –   a breach of contract, such as a default or               － 違 反 合 約，例 如 違 約 或
                 past due event;                                            逾期事件；

             –   the lender(s) of the counterparty, for                   － 交易對手的貸款人出於
                 economic or contractual reasons relating to                與交易對手財困相關的
                 the counterparty’s financial difficulty, having            經 濟 或 合 約 原 因，而 向
                 granted to the counterparty a concession(s)                交易對手授予貸款人原
                 that the lender(s) would not otherwise                     本不會考慮的優惠；
                 consider;

             –   it is becoming probable that the counterparty will       － 交易對手可能將面臨破
                 enter bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation;        產或進行其他財務重
                 or                                                         組；或

             –   the disappearance of an active market for                － 金融資產的活躍市場因
                 that financial asset because of financial                  財困而消失。
                 difficulties.

     Write-off policy                                               撇銷政策

     The Group writes off a financial asset when                    當有資料顯示債務人陷入
     there is information indicating that the debtor                嚴 重 財 困，且 並 無 實 際 收 回
     is in severe financial difficulty and there is no              的可能之時（ 包括債務人已
     realistic prospect of recovery, including when                 清 盤 或 進 入 破 產 程 序），本
     the debtor has been placed under liquidation                   集 團 會 撇 銷 金 融 資 產。已 撇
     or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings.                    銷的金融資產仍可根據本
     Financial assets written off may still be subject              集團的收回程序實施強制
     to enforcement activities under the Group’s                    執 行，在 適 當 情 況 下 考 慮 法
     recovery procedures, taking into account legal                 律 意 見。任 何 收 回 款 項 會 於
     advice where appropriate. Any recoveries made                  損益中確認。
     are recognised in profit or loss.

     Measurement and recognition of ECL                             預期信貸虧損之計量及
                                                                    確認

     The measurement of ECL is a function of the                    預期信貸虧損之計量為違
     probability of default, loss given default (i.e.               約 概 率、違 約 損 失 率（ 即 違
     the magnitude of the loss if there is a default)               約 時 之 損 失 程 度）及 違 約 風
     and the exposure at default. The assessment                    險 之 函 數。違 約 概 率 及 違 約
     of the probability of default and loss given                   損失率之評估乃基於上述
     default is based on historical data adjusted by                經前瞻性資料作出調整之
     forward-looking information as described above.                歷 史 數 據。就 金 融 資 產 之 違
     As for the exposure at default, for financial                  約 風 險 而 言，其 指 該 資 產 於
     assets, this is represented by the assets’ gross               報告日期之賬面總值。
     carrying amount at the reporting date.

     For financial assets, the ECL is estimated as the              金融資產的預期信貸虧損
     difference between all contractual cash flows                  按根據合約應付本集團的
     that are due to the Group in accordance with                   所有合約現金流量與本集
     the contract and all the cash flows that the                   團預計收取的所有現金流
     Group expects to receive, discounted at the                    量（ 按 原 定 實 際 利 率 貼 現 ）
     original effective interest rate.                              之間的差額估計。

  (c) Credit risk                                                          (c) 信貸風險
     Credit risk is the risk that a counterparty will not                       信貸風險指交易對手將未
     meet its obligations under a financial instrument                          能履行其於金融工具或客
     or customer contract, leading to a financial                               戶合約項下之責任而導致
     loss. The Group is exposed to credit risk from                             金 融 虧 損 之 風 險。本 集 團 之
     its operating activities (primarily trade and other                        經營活動（ 主要為應收貿易
     receivables, amount due from a securities broker                           賬 款 及 其 他 應 收 款 項、應 收
     and loan receivables) and from its financing                               一名證券經紀款項及應收
     activities, including deposits with banks and                              貸 款 ）以 及 融 資 活 動（ 包 括
     financial institutions. The Group’s exposure to                            於 銀 行 及 金 融 機 構 之 存 款）
     credit risk arising from cash and cash equivalents                         使 其 面 臨 信 貸 風 險。本 集 團
     is limited because the counterparties are banks                            面臨之由現金及現金等值
     and financial institutions with high credit-rating                         項目產生之信貸風險有限，
     assigned by international credit-rating agencies,                          乃由於交易對手為國際信
     for which the Group considers to have low credit                           貸評級機構指定信貸評級
     risk.                                                                      高 之 銀 行 及 金 融 機 構，因 此
                                                                                本集團認為信貸風險較低。

     The credit risk on amount due from securities                              應收證券經紀款項的信貸
     broker is limited because the counterparties                               風 險 有 限，因 為 交 易 對 手 均
     are well-established securities broker firms in                            為 香 港 知 名 證 券 經 紀 行，須
     Hong Kong which are subject to compliance                                  遵 守《證 券 及 期 貨（ 客 戶 款
     requirement under Securities and Futures (Client                           項）規則》的合規要求。
     Money) Rules.

     For financial assets whose impairment losses                               對於使用預期信貸虧損模
     a re m e a s u re d u s i n g e x p e c t e d c re d i t l o s s           型計量減值虧損的金融資
     model, the Group assesses whether their credit                             產 而 言，本 集 團 評 估 其 信 貸
     risk has increased significantly since their                               風險自初始確認後是否已
     initial recognition, and applies a three-stage                             顯 著 增 加，並 應 用 如 下 三 階
     impairment model to calculate their impairment                             段減值模型計算其減值撥
     allowance and recognise their expected credit                              備並確認其預期信貸虧損：
     losses, as follows:

     Stage 1: If the credit risk has not increased                              第1階段：倘 信 貸 風 險 自 初
              significantly since its initial recognition,                           始確認後並未顯
              the financial asset is included in stage                               著增加，則金融資
              1.                                                                     產納入第1階段。

     Stage 2: I f t h e c r e d i t r i s k h a s i n c r e a s e d             第2階段：倘 信 貸 風 險 自 初
              significantly since its initial recognition                            始確認後已顯著
              but not yet deemed to be credit-                                       增加，但尚未將其
              impaired, the financial instrument is                                  視為已發生信貸
              included in stage 2.                                                   減值，則金融工具
                                                                                     納入第2階段。

     Stage 3: If the financial instrument is credit-                            第3階段：倘 金 融 工 具 已 發
              impaired, the financial instrument is                                  生信貸減值，則金
              included in stage 3.                                                   融 工 具 納 入 第3階
                                                                                     段。

  (e) Interest rate risk                                        (e) 利率風險
     The Group’s cash flow interest rate risk primarily              本集團的現金流量利率風
     relates to variable-rate bank balances. It is the               險主要與浮動利率銀行結
     Group’s policy to keep its borrowing at floating                餘 有 關。本 集 團 之 政 策 為 保
     rate of interest so as to minimise the cash flow                持 以 浮 動 利 率 計 息 借 貸，以
     interest rate risk.                                             將現金流量利率風險減至
                                                                     最低。

     The Group’s loan receivables, lease liabilities,                本 集 團 之 應 收 貸 款、租 賃 負
     bank loans and bond payable bear interests of                   債、銀 行 貸 款 及 應 付 債 券 乃
     fixed interest rates and therefore are subject to               按 固 定 利 率 計 息，因 此 須 承
     fair value interest rate risk.                                  受公平值利率風險。

     Except as stated above, the Group does not                      除 上 述 者 外，本 集 團 於 報 告
     have other significant interest-bearing assets                  期末並無其他重大計息資
     and liabilities at the end of the reporting                     產 及 負 債，其 收 入 及 經 營 現
     period, its income and operating cash flows are                 金流量大部分不受市場利
     substantially independent of changes in market                  率變化影響。
     interest rates.

     Sensitivity analysis on cash flow interest-rate                 由於市場利率的合理可能

     possible changes in market interest rate will                   合財務報表產生重大影響，

  into the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined                    告 準 則 第13號 公 平 值 計 量 所 界
  in HKFRS 13, Fair Value Measurement. The level                          定之公平值三級架構中進行分
  into which a fair value measurement is classified                       類。公平值計量所歸類的層級乃
  is determined with reference to the observability                       經參考以下估值技術所用的輸
  and significance of the inputs used in the valuation                    入數據的可觀察性及重要性而
  technique as follows:                                                   釐定：

  • Level 1       F a i r v a l u e m e a s u re d u s i n g o n l y      • 第一級       僅以第一級輸入數據
    valuations:   Level 1 inputs, that is, unadjusted                       估值：       計 量 公 平 值，即 相 同
                  quoted prices in active markets for                                 資產或負債於計量日
                  identical assets or liabilities at the                              期之活躍市場未經調
                  measurement date.                                                   整報價。

    valuations:   inputs, that is, observable inputs                        估值：       量 公 平 值，即 未 能 符
                  which fail to meet Level 1, and not                                 合第一級規定之可觀
                  using significant unobservable inputs.                              察 輸 入 數 據，以 及 不
                  Unobservable inputs are inputs for                                  使用重大不可觀察輸
                  which market data are not available.                                入 數 據。不 可 觀 察 輸
                                                                                      入數據即不可取得市
                                                                                      場數據之輸入數據。

  • Level 3       Fair value measured using significant                   • 第三級       以重大不可觀察輸入
    valuations:   unobservable inputs                                       估值：       數據計量公平值。

  The Group’s policy is to recognise transfers into and                   本集團的政策為於事項發生或

    10. SEGMENT INFORMATION                                      10. 分部資料
        The executive directors of the Company are identified      本公司執行董事被確定為本集
        as the chief operating decision maker of the               團的主要經營決策者，以負責資
        Group for the purpose of resources allocation and          源分配及績效評估。
        performance assessments.

        The Group has three operating segments as follows:         本集團有以下三個經營分部：

        (i)   Processing and trading of used computer-related      (i)   處理及買賣二手電腦相關
              components, clothes and beauty product;                    組件、服裝及美容產品；

        (ii) Money lending services; and                           (ii) 放債服務；及

        (iii) Provision of securities brokerage, placing and       (iii) 提 供 證 券 經 紀、配 售 及 包 銷
              underwriting services.                                     服務。

        The Group’s reportable segments are strategic              本集團的可呈報分部為提供不
        business units that offer different products and           同產品及服務的策略業務單位。
        services. They are managed separately because              由於不同業務要求不同技術及
        each business requires different technology and            市場策略，故單獨管理可呈報分
        marketing strategies. The accounting policies of the       部。經營分部的會計政策與本集
        operating segments are the same as the Group’s             團會計政策相同。分部溢利或虧
        accounting policies. Segment profits or losses do not      損不包括按公平值計入損益之
        include realised and unrealised fair value gains and       金融資產之已變現及未變現公
        losses on financial assets at FVTPL and unallocated        平值收益及虧損及未分配行政
        administrative expenses, finance costs and income          開 支、融 資 成 本 及 所 得 稅 開 支。
        tax expense. Segment assets do not include financial       分部資產不包括按公平值計入
        assets at FVTPL and other unallocated corporate            損益之金融資產及其他未分配
        assets. Segment liabilities do not include unallocated     公司資產。分部負債不包括未分
        corporate liabilities.                                     配公司負債。

        As the Group plan to provide more reliable and             由於本集團計劃於財務報告提
        relevant accounting information in financial reports,      供更加可靠及相關之會計資料，
        the chief operation decision maker of the Group            本集團之首席營運決策者調整
        adjust the statistical calibre of segment report. As       分 部 報 告 之 統 計 口 徑。因 此，該
        such, the segments were combined as trading of             等分部獲合併為買賣二手電腦

        beauty product.

    19. RIGHT OF-USE ASSETS (continued)                           19. 使用權資產（ 續）
        For both years, the Group leases an office and              於兩個年度內，本集團為其業務
        warehouse for its operations. Lease contracts are           租賃辦公室及倉庫。租賃合約的
        entered into for fixed term of 2 years (2024: 2 years),     固 定 期 限 為2年（ 二 零 二 四 年：
        but may have extension and termination options as           2年），惟可有權延期及終止（ 誠
        described below. Lease terms are negotiated on an           如 下 文 所 述）。租 賃 條 款 乃 按 個
        individual basis and contain a wide range of different      別基準磋商，並包含各種不同的
        terms and conditions. In determining the lease term         條款及條件。於釐定租期及評估
        and assessing the length of the non-cancellable             不可取消期間的時長時，本集團
        period, the Group applies the definition of a contract      應用合約的定義並確定合約可
        and determines the period for which the contract is         執行的期限。
        enforceable.

        As at 31 December 2024, due to the continuous loss          於二零二四年十二月三十一日，
        of the money lending business, the management               由於放債業務持續虧損，本集團
        of the Group concluded there was impairment                 管理層認為放債業務出現減值
        indication for the money lending business and the           跡 象，為 進 行 減 值 評 估，本 集 團
        Group’s related right-of-use assets was allocated to        的相關使用權資產已分配至放
        the money lending business’s cash-generating unit           債 業 務 的 現 金 產 生 單 位（「放 債
        (the “Money Lending CGU”) for the purpose of                現 金 產 生 單 位」）。放 債 現 金 產
        impairment assessment. The recoverable amount of            生單位的可收回金額乃根據使
        the Money Lending CGU has been determined based             用價值計算法釐定，該計算法採
        on a value-in-use calculation which uses cashflow           用基於經本公司董事批准的五
        projection based on financial budgets approved by           年期財務預算的現金流量預測，
        the directors of the Company, covering a five-year          並按稅前貼現率計算得出。超過
        period with pre-tax discount rate. Cash flows beyond        五年期間之現金流量乃使用下
        that five-year period have been extrapolated using          文所載的估計增長率推斷。
        the estimated growth rate stated below.

        Key assumptions used in the calculation are as              計算時所採用的主要假設如下：
        follows:

                                                                                        2024

        Net loss margin (in average 5 years)           淨虧損率（ 平均5年）                    (0.58%)
